[Q96] Select the INCORRECTLY spelt word
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 03/07/2024 (4 t h Shift)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) yearned
(b) working
(c) worked
(d) yearns
[ANS] d
[SOL]
yearns In a relative clause, the verb is used according to the antecedent. Antecedent is the noun or pronoun that comes before a relative pronoun (who, whom, that, which). In the given sentence, the antecedent (bright young child) is singular, therefore the singular verb 'yearns' will be used. Hence, 'yearns' is the most appropriate answer.

[RULE] Foreign origin spelling (French)

(a) corporation
(b) corperate
(c) corpration
(d) corporasion

The correct spelling is 'Supersede' with an 's' at the end, not 'cede'. It is an exception to the '-cede' rule.
उदा. 'Supersede' में अंत में 's' आता है, 'cede' नहीं। यह एक अपवाद है।
[RULE] -ceed vs -cede vs -sede rule
